cua-driver-rs-v0.64.1cua-driver-rs: v0.64.1 Prerelease2026-11-27T14:25:26Z

Cua Driver 0.64.1

Summary

This release includes 1 fix.

Fixes

  • carry call identity as a typed DispatchIdentity. (#8348)

Contributors

This release contains maintainer changes only.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

Automated immutable nightly built from an exact main commit. Nightlies are opt-in, may be less stable than regular releases, and never replace stable update discovery.

Cua Driver 0.63.2

Summary

This release includes 1 fix.

Fixes

  • give browser_prepare E2E calls room for the driver's setup bounds. (#8750)

Contributors

This release contains maintainer changes only.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

DioxusLabs/dioxus ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
v0.8.0-alpha.4 Prerelease2026-11-27T14:15:28Z

Highlights

  • New dioxus-core facade. Libraries can now depend on the renderer-agnostic UI layer (VirtualDOM, rsx!, signals, hooks, stores, HTML, document) without the dioxus umbrella. Its default features cover everything except ssr and wasm-split. The VirtualDOM crate that was published as dioxus-core through 0.7 is now dioxus-vdom (#6021, #6024).
  • Pointer capture. MountedData::set_pointer_capture / release_pointer_capture let sliders, color pickers and drag-and-drop keep receiving pointer events while the pointer leaves the element. Supported on web, desktop and Dioxus Native (#6121, #6135, #6154).
  • onresize in Dioxus Native (#6152). Native is now on Blitz 0.3.0-beta.6 (#6153).
  • dx fixes for newer cargo: fingerprint busting works with cargo's new build-dir layout and build.build-dir (#6137). Projects and worktrees that share a target dir no longer clobber each other (#6140). Fat-link archiving is more robust (#6138).
  • Faster asset! compile times: const evaluation of asset metadata is about 13x faster per asset (#6111).
  • Linux binaries run on older systems: static musl dx builds are back, and the gnu builds now need glibc 2.35. install.sh picks musl automatically on older systems (#6052).
  • Android: libraries are linked with 16 KB page alignment (#6051).
  • New dioxus-timers crate: use_interval, use_timeout, use_debounce and sleep, moved here from dioxus-sdk-time, using browser timers on wasm and tokio::time elsewhere (#6162). The dioxus crate on crates.io doesn't have the timers feature that re-exports it yet, so depend on dioxus-timers directly until the next alpha.
  • dx components works again: it can clone HTTPS and SSH registries in the prebuilt binaries (#6161). cargo install dioxus-cli@0.8.0-alpha.4 builds the crates.io source, which doesn't have this fix, so prefer cargo binstall or install.sh.

Breaking changes

  • Crate renames: dioxus-core → dioxus-vdom, dioxus-core-macro → dioxus-vdom-macro, dioxus-core-template → dioxus-vdom-template (#6021). dioxus-core is now the new facade crate (#6024). dioxus::dioxus_core moves to dioxus::dioxus_vdom. dioxus::core and dioxus::core_macro keep their names. dioxus-const-vec is renamed const-array-vec (#6144), and dioxus-dx-wire-format is renamed dx-wire-format (#6055).
  • New dx-env crate: the generic dx-to-app env vars now live in dx-env. dioxus-cli-config re-exports them, so existing imports keep working (#6056).
  • Env var renames: generic DIOXUS_* env vars are renamed to DX_* (e.g. DX_DEVSERVER_IP, DX_ASSET_ROOT). dx still sets both names, and apps still read the old names as a fallback (#6057).
  • dioxus-base-types removed: event_bubbles moves to dioxus_html, and HotReloadingContext moves to dioxus_rsx_hotreload. manganis and manganis-core lose their dioxus feature, AnyFmtMarker is removed, and dioxus-html's hot-reload-context feature is renamed to hot-reload (#6054).
  • Router: dioxus-router no longer depends on dioxus-fullstack-core, and its streaming feature is removed (#6022).
  • Native pointer IDs: BlitzPointerId is now a struct with a PointerKind (#6153).
  • dioxus-web's dioxus-devtools dependency is now optional (#6028).

v4.20.0 Stable2026-11-27T08:30:17Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.19.0.

Added

  • render.py NEW.json --compare-project OLD.json (#442, phase 4): the expected change comes from the difference between the two projects, not from what the agent says it did. A changed caption cue, overlay or graphic is its own span, audio/loudness the whole sound, brand/template the whole picture, export both, and a later in on the first clip or an earlier out on the last a removed span; after the render, check.py --compare runs against OLD's output with those --expect-changed/--expect-audio-changed/--expect-removed values, and a FAIL fails the render. The result's compare_project holds the derived values, the differences they came from and check.py's document. A difference no span states (a clip added or removed, a transition, silence, fit, frame, audiogram or snap change) is refused before the first stage (project_diff_not_derivable).
  • feat: render.py --compare-project, the change scope read from two projects (#442) (#763)

What's Changed

  • feat: render.py --compare-project, the change scope read from two projects (#442) by @kajisho5 in https://github.com/kajisho5/ffmpeg-skill/pull/763

oomol-lab/open-connector ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.11.0 Stable2026-11-27T05:25:23Z

Apps that embed Open Connector can now register private providers and use scoped tokens to keep each tenant’s connections, files, and run history separate. Three provider changes need attention when upgrading from v1.10.1.

Before you upgrade

If your workflows use What to change
remove_bg Create a Leonardo.Ai API-key connection and migrate remove_bg.remove_background to leonardo_ai.remove_background. Update the input mapping and read image URLs or Base64 data from results[]. The old get_account and submit_improvement actions were also removed. Migration details
sellersprite_mcp Update V1 research action IDs, arguments, and any token grants that name them. V2 provides 28 typed research actions. Use sellersprite_mcp.list_tools to inspect current tool names and input schemas. sellersprite_mcp.call_tool remains available. If you use the proxy directly, switch its path to /v2/mcp. V2 migration
ossinsight Replace calls to ossinsight.*. The provider was removed as its upstream public API is shutting down. For historical analysis, OSSInsight provides Parquet downloads. Removal details

Embedded runtimes

  • Bring your own provider. Pass customProviders: [customCrm] to createConnectorRuntime. Import the CustomProvider type and authoring helpers from @oomol-lab/open-connector/provider. Each trusted provider gets its own service ID and uses the existing catalog, connection, and action APIs. This option is available to Node.js and Bun hosts through npm and to Cloudflare Workers deployed from source. Provider example
  • Run multiple tenants in one runtime. Give each tenant a scoped runtime token to isolate its connections, transit files, and run history. Set allowConnect: true if the tenant’s backend should also start its own connections. Existing single-tenant deployments can keep using unscoped tokens and connections. Scope setup and limits

Providers and API changes

  • Added 17 providers, including Trading 212, Zoho Mail, Yuque, ShowDoc, Salesforge, and Publora. New triggers cover four new providers and 14 existing integrations, including Trello, Box, and PayPal. Triggers
  • Gmail and Google Drive now verify account identity separately from permissions for mailbox and file operations. For example, a send-only Gmail connection can complete validation. Instagram adds Meta Unified Login. Google OAuth · Instagram setup
  • Proxies built with defineProviderProxy now return upstream headers in data.headers on provider errors. Read data.headers["retry-after"] when supplied to schedule a retry. Proxy error response

HyperFrames v0.9.102

Released on 2026-11-27.

Studio stays responsive when a preview asks for paused frames or the disk holding a history lock stops answering. The CLI reports EPIPE crashes from browser pipes, and core starts safely when the page has no body yet.

Fixes

  • CLI: Report non-stdio EPIPE crashes and guard launched browser pipes (bd477dc45, #8949).
  • Studio Server: A history lock on a disk that stops answering no longer freezes the app (281db3bd6, #8948).
  • Core: Guard runtime init and paused video observer when document.body is null (0c3bd551e, #8936).
  • Studio: Keep the editor responsive when a preview asks for paused frames (5d0d9945d, #8943).

mikubaka88/CCFA-Skills ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.13.0v0.13.0 · 按需协作与个人写作范例 Stable2026-11-27T00:22:33Z

CCFA-Skills 0.13.0 将十一月的技能、会议适配和文档更新整理为一个完整版本。18 个技能继续协同工作，写作、评审和绘图可以按任务使用已有资料，个人偏好单独保存。

这次更新

  • 按需协作：精简重复入口，保留 Humanization → Common 的共同前置。思路评审、文章评审、科研编码和实验设计继续各司其职，由主任务整合结果。
  • 个人写作范例：支持项目库与个人库、选段学习、全文八类分析、显式默认选择和继承。临时参考可以直接用于写作；需要反复使用时再登记为范例。个人论文与范例不写入公开技能包。
  • 会议适配：汇总 ACL 2027、ICML 2027、ECCV、ECOOP、IMC、ACSAC 的指南、模板和检查，以及 CVPR 2027 的模板与投稿检查。具体年份、轨道和投稿阶段分别处理。
  • 绘图保持所选设计：Python 数值图沿用选定的 JSON、代码、数据含义和指定尺寸；新建或重绘方法图保留理解内容、准备提示词、GPT Image 生成及微调流程。三语 README 换上两张当前模板渲染的分析图，展示密度、分位、状态演化和分类柱线组合，全部使用模拟数据。
  • 文档更连贯：功能说明并入对应章节，最新更新保留简短索引；原有九套数值模板、两组参考图、早期示例、来源与致谢继续保留。

安装

下载 ccfa-skills-0.13.0-lean.zip，按安装矩阵预览后更新。完整论文 PDF 使用可选的 ccfa-skills-0.13.0-papers.zip；先安装同版本 lean 包，再按需安装 papers 包。安装器检查已有个人修改，个人配置保留。

SHA256SUMS 提供两个 ZIP 的校验值，release-size-report.json 列出包体积，validation-summary.json 记录本次实际检查。模拟图一致性和本地回归不作为真实研究质量或模型性能提升的测量。

browser-use/browser-use ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
1.0.9browser-use 1.0.9 Stable2026-11-26T22:45:43Z

pip install 'browser-use==1.0.9'

  • rotate_proxy(country=None): the same Browser Use cloud browser gets a new exit IP, and its tabs, cookies and logins stay. It works over the browser's own connection with no API key; the API is only a fallback. The agent is pointed to it only when a page is a block for its IP, at most once per site. For a site that serves one country (.ru, .de, .co.uk, .kr), it's told to use an exit in that country. On 11 sites that block cloud browsers, the pass rate went from 48% to 62% (#6537, #6540).
  • rotate_proxy() now fails clearly when the cloud reports no new exit IP. A Browser(cdp_url=...) on any browser-use.com host counts as a cloud browser (#6540).
  • Claude prompt caching now works on every route: openrouter:anthropic/..., bedrock: Claude models, and ChatOpenAI(..., base_url='https://openrouter.ai/api/v1'). Before this, every step re-billed the whole prompt there (cache_read was always 0). Bedrock is covered by unit tests only and wasn't tested live (#6546, fixes #6543).

PRs: https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use/pull/6540, https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use/pull/6546

View release →

thedotmack/claude-mem ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v17.0.2 Stable2026-11-26T20:45:45Z

Setup fixes

  • Setup no longer ends in dead ends (#6501, @thedotmack). A few setup paths could never finish, or finished without syncing:
    • When cmem.ai refused a new install for a clear reason (rate limit or daily limit), the next run kept failing without asking again. That refusal is now cleared, so the next run asks again. Anything less certain keeps today's behaviour, so no second identity is made.
    • After you approved Cloud Sync, a worker that was already running without sync settings only printed a warning, so nothing synced until some later restart. Setup now waits for background work to finish (up to 5 minutes) and restarts the worker once. A worker run by your own process manager (CLAUDE_MEM_WORKER_AUTOSTART=false or CLAUDE_MEM_WORKER_URL) is left alone, and you are told to restart it there.
    • Right after checkout, setup showed the $30/month hosted plan before you had a single synced memory. That step is gone. My Work shows it as a banner you can dismiss, only after this computer's first memory syncs, and never to people who kept Pro or already have a hosted observer.
    • The "sign in later" link at the end of install started a pairing nothing ever checked, so it never worked. It is removed.
    • If you chose "This computer", My Work shows a Cloud Sync offer you can dismiss after three days.

Fixes

  • Search finds prompts that hold every word you typed (#6513, thanks @shaojunying). Prompt search matched the whole query as one exact string, so "auth bug" missed "fix the bug in the auth module". It now matches prompts that hold every term, like observation and summary search already did.
  • A reply that mentions a session limit no longer pauses capture (#6511, thanks @shaojunying). An observer reply such as "The application session limit is configurable." was read as a quota notice, so the worker paused capture and dropped that session's buffered work. Only a real limit notice at the start of the reply counts now.
  • save_memory accepts a folder path as the project (#6462, thanks @maxmad64bis). An absolute path passed as project is turned into the project name. The import refusal for an excluded project now names both causes, the folder and --project, and how to get past each.
  • Restore refuses a file that isn't a claude-mem database (#6460, thanks @ZedingZhang). A snapshot with no version stamp skipped the table check, so an unrelated SQLite file could replace your database. It now has to hold the worker tables, and your current database is left as it was.
  • The launcher recognizes more ways of writing ~/.local/bin (#6472, thanks @JayPokale). ${HOME}/.local/bin, ~/.local/bin and a trailing slash on PATH now count, so install no longer adds a duplicate line to your shell profile.
  • Server: CLAUDE_MEM_MONTHLY_TOKEN_CAP works without CLAUDE_MEM_USAGE_METERING=1 (#6514, thanks @kevincommits). With only the cap set, token usage was never recorded, so the cap was never reached. Setting the cap now records token usage.
  • OpenCode Go and OpenCode Zen presets use the new /inference addresses (#6463, thanks @maxmad64bis).
  • Updating no longer aborts while the old worker is still shutting down (#6453, thanks @maxmad64bis). The installer waited a flat 10 seconds for the old worker to exit, less than the worker's own shutdown can take, so a manual update aborted on any worker that hit its drain deadline. It now waits out the worker's full shutdown (25.5 seconds, 35.5 seconds on Windows) after one stop request.
  • Test runs on macOS no longer end in exit 134 (#6479, @thedotmack). After every test passed, a run that had loaded the embedding model crashed on exit with "A C++ exception occurred". The test setup now releases that runtime before the process exits.
  • My Work refresh no longer fails when the model skips a group (#6515, @thedotmack). A group still unreviewed after the retry failed the whole refresh, so titles, focus and the story never ran. Now the refresh carries on, says how many groups were left without a review, and asks for them again next time.

New

  • Skip Bash commands segment by segment (#6504, thanks @zhangkom). Set CLAUDE_MEM_SKIP_BASH_PATTERNS_MATCH_EVERY_SEGMENT=true to skip a compound command only when every part of it matches CLAUDE_MEM_SKIP_BASH_PATTERNS. It is off by default. See the configuration docs for the trade-off.
  • ZCode support (#6487, thanks @rochafialvin). npx claude-mem install --ide zcode adds ZCode hooks and MCP recall, and status reports a disabled or incomplete setup.
  • Per-prompt memory in OpenCode (#6454, thanks @maxmad64bis). With CLAUDE_MEM_SEMANTIC_INJECT=true, OpenCode gets the observations that best match each prompt.
  • OpenCode can use the session's own model for the observer (#6493, thanks @maxmad64bis). Set CLAUDE_MEM_OPENAI_COMPAT_MODEL_FROM_SESSION=true and the OpenAI-compatible observer uses the model picked in that OpenCode session. It is off by default.
  • Turn off the oversized-field condense call (#6516, thanks @harpreetkbadhan). Set CLAUDE_MEM_FIELD_CONDENSE_ENABLED=false to skip the model call that shortens an oversized tool field; the field then goes straight to head and tail trimming. It is on by default, so nothing changes unless you set it.
  • Rapid-MLX, Straitly and TeamoRouter presets for the OpenAI-compatible provider (#6502, thanks @raullenchai; #6498, thanks @HashimSyedUBC; #6451, thanks @2867297362mjb-sketch). The TeamoRouter docs state what its privacy policy says it records.
  • Docs: a verified Kiro MCP recall guide (#6473, thanks @rochafialvin), a note on how CLAUDE_MEM_DEDUP_ENABLED affects CLAUDE_MEM_REINFORCE_ALPHA (#6461, thanks @maxmad64bis), and a note that CLAUDE_MEM_OBSERVER_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS also sizes the condense request (#6518, thanks @harpreetkbadhan).

Contributors

Thank you to @shaojunying, @maxmad64bis, @ZedingZhang, @JayPokale, @kevincommits, @zhangkom, @rochafialvin, @raullenchai, @HashimSyedUBC, @harpreetkbadhan and @2867297362mjb-sketch for the work in this release.

Full changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v17.0.1...v17.0.2

v17.0.1 Stable2026-11-26T18:46:57Z

Critical fixes

  • The setup page shows your cmem.ai device code, and reopening after it expires gets a fresh one (#6500, @thedotmack). After you pick Cloud Sync and start the free trial, cmem.ai asks for a device code. That code was only printed in the terminal, which most people never look at because setup runs in the browser. The checkout step now shows Your device code with a copy button, next to "Waiting for this device to connect…". If the code expired, Reopen sent you back to a dead link with the same expired code. It now starts a new pairing with a new code. The code is never put in a URL, log line or analytics event, and the page reads it from a route only this machine can reach.
  • Windows: My Work reads no longer hang when a recent Bash command holds regex-shaped text (#6471, thanks @zhangkom, closes #6470 reported by @ingermaa). Shell text such as s.replace(/\r\n/g, ' ') was taken for an absolute path. Resolving /\r\n/g, could produce a UNC network path, and project discovery stalled while Windows tried a network lookup. Text that starts with a slash and a backslash is now ignored on Windows. Drive paths and POSIX paths are read as before.
  • Windows: OMP capture and Screenpipe saves reach the worker again (#6481, thanks @AyhamJo7, refs #6477 reported by @Jumpstile). On Windows the OMP hook and the Screenpipe skill posted to port 37777 by default. The worker picks its default port from a hash of the data folder, so on a default Windows install nothing listened on 37777 and OMP sessions were not recorded, with no error shown. Both now work out the default port the same way the worker does. A port saved in settings.json is still used first, and macOS and Linux are unchanged. OMP users on Windows: run npx claude-mem install --ide omp once after updating. The OMP hook is a copied file, so an update alone does not replace it.

Fixes

  • Claude provider: condensing an oversized field now follows CLAUDE_MEM_OBSERVER_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS (#6508, thanks @0xABCD01, closes #6503 reported by @JonT6). The setting sized the condense request on the HTTP providers, but the Claude call never passed it. The request asked for the full 12,800 characters, and the extra output was billed and then clipped. At the default setting the Claude condense request now asks for 8,192 characters, the same as the HTTP providers. The Agent SDK has no per-request output cap, so on this path the setting sizes the request and is not a hard limit.
  • My Work keeps a cut-off answer for diagnosis, and Focus can skip a request it cannot place (#6441, thanks @0xABCD01, part of #6432 reported by @hello-sebastian). When an API answer ran out of tokens, its text was discarded and the error read as "raise the limit". The partial answer is now saved under failed-answers/, the step fails with that file's path first, and the error says a run-out almost always means the model's output degenerated, which a higher limit does not fix. The focus step now accepts an ignored list with a reason. An ignored request goes to Other, where you can move it.
  • The SessionStart timeline lists one turn's observations oldest first (#6495, thanks @HEOJUNFO). Turns ran oldest to newest, but the observations inside one turn ran newest to oldest, so the last row was not the newest entry. The newest entry is now last, as the timeline's own instructions say.
  • claude-mem server jobs retry no longer reports requeued while a worker still holds the job (#6494, thanks @HEOJUNFO). Cancelling a processing job and retrying it before the provider call returned left the job queued for good, and it never ran. The command now sets the job back to failed with retry_republish_failed, prints the reason and exits 1. Run it again after the provider call has returned and the job is requeued and runs.
  • Server: CLAUDE_MEM_MONTHLY_REQUEST_CAP works without CLAUDE_MEM_USAGE_METERING=1 (#6489, thanks @kevincommits). With only the cap set, no requests were counted, so the cap was never reached and no request was answered with 402. Setting the cap now also counts requests. CLAUDE_MEM_MONTHLY_TOKEN_CAP still needs metering on.
  • Server: GET /v1/jobs answers 400 for a since that is not a date (#6484, thanks @kevincommits). A value such as last-week or 2026-13-45 dropped the filter and returned jobs from every date. It now answers 400 INVALID_DATE. A day that is not on the calendar, such as 2026-02-30, also gets a 400, and epoch milliseconds are now accepted.
  • OpenClaw: uninstalling gives the memory slot back to the plugin that had it (#6445, thanks @DeryFerd). Installing took plugins.slots.memory from another memory plugin with no record of it, and uninstalling deleted the slot. Install now remembers the previous owner and uninstall restores it. When there was no previous owner, the slot is deleted as before.
  • Install: the marketplace manifest and package.json are written atomically (#6475, thanks @DeryFerd). A crash, a killed process or a full disk in the middle of a write could leave a truncated file. These were the last two plain writes in the install flow.
  • My Work names the event type when it drops an unreadable stream frame (#6476, thanks @DeryFerd). A frame that was not JSON was dropped without a trace, so a feed could stop updating with nothing to say why. The browser console now gets a warning that names the event type.
  • Messages on Mac: someone leaving a group now reaches the transcript (#6509, thanks @chuenchen309). The Mac source skipped the leave rows in chat.db. They now read "(Ben left the group)", the same as from the Photon source. Group photo and background changes are still skipped.

Contributors

Thank you to everyone whose work is in this release: @zhangkom, @AyhamJo7, @0xABCD01, @HEOJUNFO, @kevincommits, @DeryFerd and @chuenchen309 for the fixes, and @ingermaa, @Jumpstile, @JonT6 and @hello-sebastian for the reports.

Full changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v17.0.0...v17.0.1

Graphify-Labs/graphify ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.121 Stable2026-11-26T19:38:53Z

A large batch across community pull requests: extractor accuracy (C#, TypeScript, Rust, COBOL, Python), node-id and edge correctness on incremental rebuilds, a self-contained Canvas renderer for large graphs, and a wave of byte-identical performance work. The C#, TypeScript, Rust, COBOL and Python changes alter node/edge output, so the AST cache schema bumps (56 to 57) and those languages re-extract on the first build after upgrading; everything else needs no rebuild.

Features

  • serve/viz graph.html gains a self-contained Canvas 2D renderer for large graphs: above the vis-network comfort threshold (and within --node-limit/GRAPHIFY_VIZ_NODE_LIMIT) it emits a deterministic, community-packed Canvas view with viewport culling and sparse labels at low zoom, embedded as a base64 payload with no external scripts. Small graphs keep the existing vis-network output (#5521, thanks @GwungTz).

Extractor fixes

  • csharp a call through a namespace-qualified receiver (Acme.Audio.Mixer.Instance.Play(), Acme.Audio.Mixer.Reset(), or a var m = Acme.Audio.Mixer.Instance; m.Play() chain, plus null-conditional forms) resolves through the named type the same way the short name under using already did, binding the exact namespace and failing closed on an unknown or ambiguous path (#5473, thanks @aliyapici).
  • typescript a named object type alias (type Port = { read(): number }, including an unqualified Readonly<{...}>) retains its method signatures as method nodes so a typed-receiver call resolves to them, while the signatures stay out of bare-call, static-call and module-symbol lookup (#5455, thanks @rvitaliy).
  • typescript an extensionless import of a declaration file (./types/api, or a tsconfig paths alias) resolves to the adjacent .d.ts/index.d.ts instead of a sourceless stub, with a real implementation file still winning the guess (#5472, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).
  • rust a use of a module path or a grouped self (use krate::m::{self, X}, use crate::a::b) resolves to the real module-file node (m.rs/m/mod.rs) instead of a sourceless bare-name stub, with external-crate paths still failing closed (#5398, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).
  • python a function-local import's shadow climbs every enclosing function scope, so a bare call in a nested def is still shadowed by an outer function's import instead of falling back to a same-named module-level twin (#5421 follow-up, thanks @ayushcodes10).
  • cobol a PERFORM A THRU Z links every paragraph in the range (not only the endpoints, and nothing outside it); a procedure copybook's own paragraphs and intra-copybook PERFORMs are extracted; and a PERFORM between a program and its copybook resolves cross-file, fail-closed on an unknown or ambiguous target (#5424, #5425, #5426, thanks @pavansystems).

Correctness fixes

  • incremental same-stem files (math.c/math.h, index.ts/index.js) keep the same node ids on a changed-files rebuild as on a full build: the colliding files are re-extracted together so disambiguation sees the whole group, and a deleted sibling drops its partner back to the bare id (#5464, thanks @0xkaushik-ai).
  • extract the XAML code-behind class no longer keeps an absolute-checkout-path id (or duplicates) when only the .xaml file is re-extracted; out-of-batch source files are canonicalized in the id-remap (#5466, thanks @0xkaushik-ai).
  • extract the decl/def fold's edge de-dup is scoped to the merged ids, so folding a header/impl pair no longer drops unrelated edges (a recursive self-call, or same-stem .cu/.metal member edges) order-dependently (#5468, thanks @0xkaushik-ai).
  • watch the event path is resolved so a manual observer matches events under a symlinked root (#5498, thanks @madhusudanpatnaik).
  • build a different-relation edge winner no longer inherits the replaced edge's also_seen and score (#5490, thanks @poberherr).
  • analyze graph_diff handles mixed int/str node IDs and MultiGraph parallel edges instead of crashing or overwriting (#5512, thanks @Gaurav-meena95).
  • cargo a member crate's dependency.workspace = true resolves inherited deps against the workspace manifest dir rather than the scan root (#5496, thanks @madhusudanpatnaik).
  • graphdb a node with a null file_type or an edge with a null relation no longer crashes label/relation formatting (#5497, thanks @madhusudanpatnaik).

Performance (output byte-identical)

  • build per-file stems and alias work are cached with a sentinel map in _alias_index, and edge-endpoint suffixes are memoized per source_file (#5492, #5494, thanks @madhusudanpatnaik).
  • paths large top-level JSON lists are written in C-encoded chunks for lower peak memory (#5493, thanks @madhusudanpatnaik).
  • report edge and node scans are folded into single passes with a memoized _real_node (#5495, thanks @madhusudanpatnaik).
  • detect ignore, symlink and paper checks are sped up (3000-char paper read, lexical relative-path containment, a POSIX non-symlink fastpath) (#5499, thanks @madhusudanpatnaik).
  • cluster score_all is computed in a single O(|E|) pass without per-community SubGraphViews, and canonical edges are passed straight to native Leiden without rebuilding the stable graph (#5510 thanks @Gaurav-meena95, #5401 thanks @Max17190).
  • export to_graphml fast-paths XML sanitization and cleans nodes/edges in one pass, and the NetworkX loader releases graph rows as it consumes them (#5511 thanks @Gaurav-meena95, #5434 thanks @Max17190).
  • callflow archetype keywords are counted in one word-scan pass instead of a regex per keyword (#5433, thanks @Max17190).

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v4.0.43CC Switch v4.0.43 Stable2026-11-26T15:29:48Z

CC Switch v4.0.43

本版本为授权中心增加了「自动查询额度」开关：关掉后，官方账号的额度只在你点刷新时才查，适合经代理上网、不想在代理就绪前直连官方服务的情况。托盘里也能直接切换 Antigravity 官方账号，每个账号旁边显示剩余额度。「应用」页现在认得 OpenCode 2，能正确检查更新、升级和卸载；OpenCode 2.4 的会话也能显示助手回复了。用量统计的速度只算成功的请求，并剔除中转缓冲后一次性吐出的异常值。

修复包括：先用 Chat Completions 供应商、再在直连模式下切回官方 ChatGPT 时 Codex 会话无法继续，macOS 每次退出后菜单栏图标位置被重置，~/.gemini/.env 里带引号的值，带块注释的 OpenClaw 配置被写坏，删除环境变量后 shell 配置丢了末尾换行，以及 Pi 的插件识别和更新问题。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

授权中心「自动查询额度」开关

（#9325，见 #8990）

打开授权中心、当前官方卡定时刷新、鼠标悬停托盘，都会自动向官方服务查询账号额度。经代理上网时，如果代理还没就绪，这些请求就会直连出去。现在授权中心顶部「登录时自动打开浏览器」下方多了一个「自动查询额度」开关，默认打开。

  • 关掉后：Claude、ChatGPT（Codex）、xAI、Kimi、Antigravity、GitHub Copilot、WorkBuddy、CodeBuddy 等官方账号的额度只在你点账号行的 ↻ 或「全部刷新」时查询。打开授权中心、卡片定时刷新、悬停托盘都不再自动发请求，已查到的结果照常显示。
  • 没查过的账号：显示「点刷新查询额度」和刷新按钮，不会是一片空白。
  • 启动时：设置读出来之前不查额度，关了开关的用户不会在启动那一刻漏出一次请求。
  • 不受影响的：第三方供应商的余额脚本照常刷新；DeepSeek 账号的余额和登录状态一起显示，也不受这个开关影响。

另外，「全部刷新」现在会刷新页面上所有账号（关掉自动查询时也一样）；WorkBuddy、CodeBuddy 还会跳过后台 5 分钟的缓存，拿到最新数字。

托盘切换 Antigravity 官方账号

托盘里多了一个 Antigravity 分区，列出授权中心里保存的 Antigravity 账号，每个账号旁边显示剩余额度（按 Gemini、Claude/GPT、Argon 分组，取每组最低的一档）。

  • 勾选的是 agy 当前登录的账号。点另一个账号即切换，和在授权中心切换一样写入系统钥匙串；agy 正在运行时，会提示已打开的会话重启后生效。
  • 当前账号的额度随托盘一起刷新，其他账号最多每 5 分钟刷新一次，各用各的登录，不需要先切过去。在授权中心查到的额度也会同步到托盘。
  • 「管理账号…」打开授权中心；切换失败时也会打开授权中心。
  • 授权中心里没有保存 Antigravity 账号时，不显示这个分区，也不会读钥匙串。

「应用」页支持 OpenCode 2

（覆盖 #8727）

OpenCode 2 是一个单独的 npm 包（@opencode/cli，Homebrew 是 anomalyco/tap/opencode-v2），和 1.x 的 opencode-ai 并存。以前「应用」页只认 opencode-ai，装了 OpenCode 2 的用户会遇到：

  • 最新版本取自 1.x，比本地的 2.x 还低，所以永远提示不了 2.x 的更新；
  • 升级时装的是 opencode-ai@latest：npm 会报 EEXIST 失败，Windows 上的 pnpm 则不报错，却把 opencode 换成了一个没装好的 1.x，命令直接坏掉；
  • 卸载不认 npm 和 opencode-v2 装的版本，还会留下安装脚本建的 opencode2 别名。

现在 CC Switch 按实际安装位置判断是 1.x 还是 2.x，更新检查、升级命令、npm 安装脚本设置和 Node 版本要求都用对应的包；卸载能找到所属的 npm 包（包括 WSL），认得 opencode-v2，只在 opencode2 确实是安装脚本建的时候才删它。新安装仍然装 1.x，不会替你在两条线之间切换；用 Volta 装的分不出来，仍按 1.x 处理。

变更

  • 速度统计更准确（#9952，关闭 #9950）：直通的流式响应按各协议（Anthropic、OpenAI Responses、OpenAI Chat、Gemini）识别真正的第一个输出 token，不再把元数据事件当成首字。速度和首字时间只算成功（2xx）的请求，超过 10,000 tok/s 的视为中转缓冲后一次性吐出，不算速度，请求详情里会说明原因。按供应商、模型汇总的速度仍是总输出 token 除以总生成时间。
  • Claude 会话日志的耗时从实际发出请求算起：请求卡住时，Claude Code 会重发同一个请求。以前没开路由时，导入的耗时和首字时间从触发这次回复的消息算起，把卡住的那段（常常十几分钟）也算了进去，拉低了估算速度。较新的 Claude Code 会记下每次实际发出请求的时间，现在从那一刻算起。旧版日志照旧计算，已经导入的记录不变。
  • 用量统计的应用筛选和侧栏顺序一致（#10006，关闭 #10005）：包括在「应用」页拖过的顺序。
  • Codex「保留官方 Fast 档」说明（见 #10000）：卡片没有模型列表时，这个开关打开了也存不进去，现在会锁住并提示先在模型列表里加上 GPT 模型。说明里也写明：Codex 桌面版要登录 ChatGPT 才显示 Fast，用第三方卡时请打开设置里的「非接管切换时保留官方登录」（或使用路由模式），请求仍发往这张卡；命令行版不需要登录（/fast）。
  • 插件页显示所属项目（#9963，关闭 #9962）：Claude Code 的 local / project 插件在行下方显示所属项目目录，也能按目录搜索。删除多个项目的同名插件后不会再残留删不掉的行。
  • Linux 支持系统默认终端（#9987，关闭 #9986）：「打开终端」在已知的终端都找不到时，会再依次尝试 x-terminal-emulator 和 xdg-terminal-exec，首选终端仍排在最前。
  • Skills 页 Pi 列的说明（#9971）：改为说明 Pi 也会读 ~/.agents/skills，那里的技能没勾选也能直接用。

修复

  • 直连模式下 Codex 会话切回官方 ChatGPT 后无法继续（见 #9054）：先用 Chat Completions 供应商时，转换出来的思考条目 ID 是 rs_<response id>。部分上游的回复 ID 正好是 32 位十六进制，形状和官方后端会去查找的 ID 一样，在直连模式下切回官方 ChatGPT 后报 Item with id 'rs_resp_...' not found，会话无法继续。现在 ID 末尾加上序号，官方后端不会再去查它。已经存在旧会话里的条目 ID 不会变，这些会话在直连模式下仍然会报错，请新开会话。
  • macOS 菜单栏图标位置每次退出后被重置（见 #6631）：从托盘退出、关闭主窗口退出和重启时，CC Switch 都会先移除托盘图标，而 macOS 移除时会删掉你按住 ⌘ 拖到的位置，Hidden Bar 这类工具的分组也随之失效。macOS 上不再主动移除，进程退出时系统会自己收走图标。
  • OpenCode 2.4 的会话不显示助手回复（见 #9982）：OpenCode 2.4 把助手回复的各段内容存进了新的 session_part 表，会话管理以前只读旧表，只看得到你的提问。现在两张表合起来读，搜索和删除会话也包括新表。
  • ~/.gemini/.env 里带引号的值（#9928，关闭 #9926）：Gemini CLI 官方文档就是用引号写的，以前导入后 Key 和地址会带着引号和行尾注释，路由也把它们原样发给上游。现在和 Gemini CLI 一样去掉。
  • OpenClaw 配置里有块注释时被写坏（#10002，关闭 #10001）：openclaw.json 里有 /* ... */ 时，CC Switch 一写就把结尾的 / 弄丢，文件再也读不出来。
  • 删除冲突的环境变量后 shell 配置丢了末尾换行（#9929，关闭 #9927）：之后再往文件末尾追加一行，会和最后一行粘在一起。现在除了删掉的那一行，其他内容（包括 CRLF 换行和末尾换行）原样保留。
  • 通过 Vite+ 安装的 Pi 识别不出插件（#9976，见 #9960）：从桌面启动的 CC Switch 拿不到 shell 配置里的 VP_HOME 等变量，pi list 实际跑成了 vp list。现在会从登录 shell 补上 Vite+ 用到的几个目录变量。
  • Pi 官方安装器装的版本点更新会再用 npm 装一份（#10015，关闭 #10013）：现在改用 Pi 自己的 pi update。
  • Antigravity 卡换号失败时，云同步可能上传了没生效的绑定（#9978）：WebDAV / S3 现在会等换号完成或回滚后再取快照。

致谢

感谢 @funkpopo（自动查询额度开关、Skills 说明）、@allenxu09（自动查询额度开关、Pi 安装与插件、Antigravity 云同步）、@SnowGlow-aww（速度统计）、@HEOJUNFO（Gemini .env、OpenClaw 块注释、shell 配置换行）、@jimpablo（插件页项目目录、用量筛选顺序）、@liusheng2020（Linux 终端）的贡献。

特别感谢 @xwil1（#8727 发现并修了 OpenCode 2 的版本检查和升级命令）和 @luantu（#6631 指出 macOS 菜单栏图标位置丢失的原因），本版本的修复沿用了他们的思路。

感谢 @wlc534、@Duosl、@crazyi、@tomorrow1109、@BWrong、@lonelyman0108、@yyw-code 报告问题和提供复现信息。

完整的技术细节请见 CHANGELOG。
